## Nov 17 — ACTUAL owner 24-hour reminder truthfulness fix, Tulsa DST-safe native SQLite write (PR270 SOURCE ONLY)

**New actual app+server bug fixed:** the saved `appointmentReminder` says “your service is tomorrow” but previous owner preparation accepted **any future scheduled** job, including next week. Real owner `MessageComposer`, shared `customerAppointmentTemplateReadiness` and authenticated `POST /api/customer-messages` now insist the job is scheduled for **tomorrow by America/Chicago calendar**, even when the owner customizes the template heading/body. The native SQL `INSERT OR IGNORE` also uses the exact Tulsa midnight deadline `AND julianday('now') < julianday(?)` and the existing same-write exact scheduledAt/contact guards, so a slow operation cannot save a misleading reminder after midnight. ## Nov 17 — customer appointment messages cannot confirm past jobs (REAL SOURCE FIX; NO DEPLOY)

On existing **DRAFT PR #270**, corrected the actual `customerAppointmentTemplateReadiness` guard ([logic `1b95940`](https://github.com/kylebrooks86/first-in-response-website/commit/1b9594071e317b29f2e9f038827554c3c5e94d1b)): a saved job still marked **scheduled** with its time already elapsed could previously pass template readiness and prepare a misleading appointment confirmation/reminder. The guard now requires a valid **future** scheduled time and a valid clock for any saved template containing `@schedule_date`; explicitly directs the owner to verify stale job status. Nonappointment custom messages remain available. [Actual MessageComposer click-time fix `0a1ebce`](https://github.com/kylebrooks86/first-in-response-website/commit/0a1ebce4d30d935227c7710990fea74824e20217) revalidates immediately before the durable prepared-message POST even if the modal was opened before the appointment elapsed. No automatic customer communication, money, or database write in this change.

## NOV 15 — ACTUAL GATE7 FULL D1 RECORDS REVIEW SOURCE, NOT CORE-ONLY (NO DEPLOY)

- **Concrete functional gap advanced**: old default-OFF `/api/backup/numbered-v2` captured 23 numbered-core tables, blocked optional properties/locations and could not include supplemental operational/migration records. New `shared/lib/complete-d1-records-review.mjs` performs ONE read-only transactional `D1.batch` of the reviewed 23-core + mandatory `auth_rate_limits` and `d1_migrations`, plus ALL declared optional property / service-location history tables when their explicit schema selectors are on. Unknown tables, missing optional tables, forged counts, incomplete/unsafe data and unknown provider metadata abort without partial archive. Provider-internal only tables are excluded only under exact verified DDL.
- **Current authoritative Cloudflare Oct 30, 2026 limit** says D1.batch counts as **ONE Worker invocation query**, not one per contained SELECT; the old 49-statement ceiling was unnecessarily restrictive. Official https://developers.cloudflare.com/d1/platform/limits/ and https://developers.cloudflare.com/d1/worker-api/d1-database/. Batch still incurs actual row reads on Free and protects no paid plan; no provider query was issued here.

## NOV 15 — GATE7 FINAL PAIRED RECOVERY SHA READS PINNED TO VERIFIED DESCRIPTORS (NEW ACTUAL CODE)

- **Concrete distinct Gate7 source integrity defect fixed:** `rehearse_protected_complete_records_and_photos.mjs` used `lstat(path)`, independently reopened `createReadStream(path)`, then `lstat(path)` again to hash the SAVED encrypted records, SAVED encrypted photo archive, and actual restored SQLite database before issuing final paired recovery evidence. Temporary path substitution, or same-size content mutation without checking timestamps, could be missed by this earlier path-only check even if actual bytes hashed were of a different inode. Not a demonstrated owner attack or data loss.
- **Actual unmerged PR270 implementation** commit [`b04fdc29d2ca8d04c8cc63092490d3adbc04612b`](https://github.com/kylebrooks86/first-in-response-website/commit/b04fdc29d2ca8d04c8cc63092490d3adbc04612b) opens the underlying regular file, verifies that the held descriptor matches the original resolved path by filesystem device/inode/size/mtime/ctime **before streaming**, hashes via that SAME held descriptor with `handle.createReadStream({autoClose:false})`, then checks both descriptor and pathname identities after reading. A source changed or substituted during the final proof must fail closed. This narrows, but cannot remove every possible concurrent filesystem race, and does not establish an atomic backup or real off-device restore.
- **Focused synthetic offline tests** commit [`1686dac6fd0d89ab518b3e7b87a9e59393287fd3`](https://github.com/kylebrooks86/first-in-response-website/commit/1686dac6fd0d89ab518b3e7b87a9e59393287fd3) extends the existing real SQLite offline recovery job with exact encrypted-format byte digest, symlink denial, absent source, overlimit refusal and same-size changed ciphertext digest. ## NOV 15 — ENCRYPTED SAVED RECORD/PHOTO RECEIPT NOW PINS ACTUAL READ FILE DESCRIPTORS — SOURCE CI, NOT GATE7 ACCEPTANCE

- **Actual newly fixed local file-substitution risk:** previous saved encrypted backup pair receipt code used `lstat(path)`, independently opened `createReadStream(path)` / `readFile(path)`, then `lstat(path)` again. A transient path replacement between these checks could read a different inode without being identified solely by the before/after pathname metadata. `business-app-parity/protected_recovery_pair_receipt.mjs` now pins each data hash and receipt read to the *actual opened file descriptor*, verifies dev/ino/size/mtime/ctime against original lstat **before data read**, and checks *both* held descriptor and pathname after read. The input data is never rewritten. This narrows, but does not universally eliminate, all concurrent changes or establish an atomic original cloud/device snapshot.
- **Implementation:** Draft main-based PR #270 source commit [`9b0f60d1ff7935b39d1bda3c928497ccf13737bf`](https://github.com/kylebrooks86/first-in-response-website/commit/9b0f60d1ff7935b39d1bda3c928497ccf13737bf); focused synthetic regression [`94c5972cde0696addfe2cdac5950b295e913852c`](https://github.com/kylebrooks86/first-in-response-website/commit/94c5972cde0696addfe2cdac5950b295e913852c), including symlink receipt rejection, malformed/missing owner path fail-close, existing receipt no-overwrite. **Real GitHub CI** at source/test SHA `94c5972c`: [isolated complete SQLite recovery #43776161889](https://github.com/kylebrooks86/first-in-response-website/actions/runs/43776161889) SUCCESS (including targeted receipt step); [exact website/calculator tree #43776161959](https://github.com/kylebrooks86/first-in-response-website/actions/runs/43776161959) SUCCESS; [independent website verifier #43776161968](https://github.com/kylebrooks86/first-in-response-website/actions/runs/43776161968) SUCCESS. Overall 26-group matrix pending at first record; preserve exact-code and docs-only HEAD distinction.
- **Grounded status and boundary:** local encryption/custody receipt verification is stronger, but real paired complete owner records+supplemental-table data AND actual photo bytes are **not** now present in verified off-device independent custody, nor physically restored into isolated DR/NEW LIVE; Gate7 **OPEN**. Already accepted two DARK Worker versions, D1 IDs and URL-off proof, original DR 26/26, QA screenshots/iPhone, TEST Stripe disabled mode/isolated DB/Access 302 and webhook GET 405, and failed revoked Access READ token remain accepted. No redundant retests or Cloudflare/Acer/phone input needed for this code patch. **No Cloudflare/Stripe/Access/SQL/D1/Worker/R2 or paid services/merge/DNS/payment/production deployment** were touched. Formal gates G1/G2/G3/G8 **4/10 accepted**; G4/G5/G6/G7/G9/G10 OPEN; NEW LIVE **DARK NO-GO**.

## NOV 15 — ENCRYPTED RECORDS/PHOTO PAIR RECEIPT SAME-SIZE REPLACEMENT GUARD — SYNTHETIC VERIFICATION, NOT OFF-DEVICE RESTORE

- **Actual Gate7 source integrity fix** `business-app-parity/protected_recovery_pair_receipt.mjs`: `fingerprint(file,sizeMax,magic)` already refused initial symlink, oversize, wrong magic and digest mismatch, but did **not re-stat after streaming**, leaving a gap for a saved encrypted file that is replaced or modified during hashing without changing its length. Now the routine obtains a **post-read `lstat`**, and rejects missing/non-regular/symlink, different filesystem device/inode, file size, `mtimeMs` or `ctimeMs`, as well as incorrect encrypted magic. It still recomputes SHA-256 over actual byte stream. This is a local consistency guard, NOT a guaranteed atomic filesystem snapshot or proof of off-device custody.
- **New completely synthetic offline test** `test_protected_recovery_pair_receipt.mjs`: starts with fake opaque `FIRERB01` and `FIREPX01` byte envelopes, seals and independently inspects a saved pair, verifies **both exact digests**, refuses a second create via native exclusive `EEXIST` (without deleting existing receipt), rejects **same-length byte modifications** to records and photo files, symlink source substitution and any edited receipt claiming owner off-device custody. All temp files are deleted in `finally`; **no real owner backups, passphrases, Cloudflare/Stripe/SQL or provider data used**. The test does **not deterministically simulate concurrent replacement**; post-read inode/timestamp guard is code review plus normal runtime coverage, not a simulated race-proof claim.
- **Actual source commits:** receipt guard `c78dc1aefee5e33fa35f7f3fadd94c2ea03da5bb`; synthetic file test `3a9c99e60d8705111f3a0fede0ca0564b65186c0`; focused CI `728d59fa1bf740040792f107e92839f687369219`; exact immutable checksum re-pin in public-site CI `dfc003f1e5b3aed7dcdc9a269c539b0df3c7cba0`. First new test-only run failed because native exclusive `EEXIST` was (correctly) returned but the test asserted only FIRE's custom error prefix; **production source denial was right, test expectation corrected** `176bf96999e4dbc30a86551905ae2df69ab9cac9`, no loosening of exclusive protection. ## NOV 15 — NEW Gate7 source change: bound owner records restore upload before allocation (CI PENDING)

- **Distinct actual bug fixed on current main-based draft PR270:** `shared/app/api/backup/route.ts` formerly did `await request.text()` before comparing the in-memory string to 5 MB. A large HTTP upload could therefore be allocated before its claimed limit was enforced. The new source-only `shared/lib/bounded-records-restore.mjs` enforces a **5,000,000-byte streaming limit**, checks an untrusted Content-Length without relying upon it, refuses split-chunk oversize, malformed UTF-8, interrupted/empty input with 400/413 no-store and no restore writes, and only delivers bounded text to JSON parser. Includes TS declaration; existing owner/auth, numbered restore fail-closed, signed-deposit/refund/immutable record recovery logic retained.

## November 13 NEW LIVE physical D1 schema inventory compatibility fix

Identified a genuine pre-activation blocker: `new-live-first-job-preflight.mjs` used `name NOT LIKE 'sqlite_%'`, whose underscore wildcard can hide user-created `sqliteX_*` tables; it also rejected Cloudflare's documented `_cf_METADATA` / `_cf_KV` internal tables even when correctly defined. This could falsely block a legitimate independently provisioned NEW LIVE D1, or worse silently omit unreviewed durable records from a clean-start assessment. Fixed `a516deeba9ebb1cb283ad43e0fd427f1a026e6cf`: only literal `sqlite_` prefix is excluded, and only exact documented Cloudflare metadata DDL is allowed; spoofed provider metadata and any other extra table block first-use. ## November 13, 2026 — actual 26-migration isolated D1 restore, Job # and refund continuity; PR #259

**Current integrated, source-only development draft:** [PR #259](https://github.com/kylebrooks86/first-in-response-website/pull/259), based on the already advanced [PR #257](https://github.com/kylebrooks86/first-in-response-website/pull/257) source/continuity SHA `a27b13abaae51dddd81182b810d8667bc082ec82`. #257 combines independent DR/NEW LIVE additive 26th permanent Customer #/Job # D1 migration with earlier exact 25 baseline migrations, immutable triggers, and full supplemental/core SQLite local recovery fixes. **True release-blocking functional defect corrected:** The offline complete-record restore previously demanded *every* supplemental table in the prepared isolated destination be empty. A properly migrated DR/NEW LIVE D1 necessarily already contains the `d1_migrations` history (26 ordered entries). Consequently a valid, independently migrated clean schema would refuse a future customer-data restore; alternatively manually replaying journal entries would fabricate migration execution. New actual `restore_numbered_records_to_isolated_sqlite.mjs` explicitly permits **only** this existing journal table to be populated in the otherwise empty schema; inside the SQLite recovery transaction it requires complete exact archive-vs-target equality of all journal columns and data, ordered IDs/names from the reviewed 26 source migrations, and unchanged AUTOINCREMENT high-water. **Additional actual data-loss defect corrected in core restore:** Previously `customers`, `estimates`, `payments`, `payment_refunds` and other mandatory core rows could omit a legitimate nullable schema column, pass the subset-key insertion and field comparison, and restore that detail as NULL/DEFAULT. Now **every actual destination schema column of each core row** must be present, mirroring PR #255's previously integrated supplemental-field protection. Missing customer email or refund provider ID fails closed before an incomplete dataset can be returned.

**Actual updated executable recovery regression:** `business-app-parity/test_migrated_d1_journal_recovery.mjs` constructs the real isolated SQLite schema by applying all 25 reviewed baseline migration SQL files plus the identical independent #0025 SQL, populates 26 applied migration journal rows and realistic AUTOINCREMENT high-water, and then saves synthetic Customer #0001, Job #0001, a completed estimate, paid $30 balance, a negative $5 refund payment and matching refund journal. It exports the exact full locally isolated SQL archive, then restores it into a separately migrated blank database without touching its preexisting D1 journal; verifies customer email, permanent Job #0001, paid/refund cents, exact journal names/timestamps and high-water, FK integrity, and the next unrelated estimate allocating Job #0002. Negatives reject forged source/destination refund migration entries, missing/empty migration journal, changed journal sequence, occupied destination, missing customer email, and missing refund provider reference, always without leaving partial recovered databases. Uses only disposable local SQLite, synthetic records, Node 22 built-in SQLite, and zero Cloudflare/Stripe calls.
